主席報告
CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
本人欣然向各位股東提呈華潤萬象生活有限公                    I am pleased to present to shareholders the business review and
司（「本公司」）及其附屬公司（統稱「本集團」）截                outlook of China Resources Mixc Lifestyle Services Limited (the
至二零二四年六月三十日止六個月業績回顧與                    “Company”) and its subsidiaries (collectively, the “Group”) for the
展望。                                     six months ended 30 June 2024.

上 半 年，面 對 國 際 局 勢 複 雜 嚴 峻、國 內 結 構 調     During the first half of the year, despite multiple pressures
整 深 化 的 多 重 壓 力，中 國 經 濟 穩 中 有 進、質 效     under the complex and challenging international situation as
提 升。中 國 政 府 高 度 重 視 擴 大 內 需，增 強 市 場     well as the deepening of domestic structural adjustment,
信心，消費政策有力促進與長效機制完善並舉，                   China’s economy has been developing in a steady manner with
實 現 消 費 規 模 擴 大、消 費 場 景 更 新，消 費 主 引     improved quality and efficiency. The Chinese government
擎 作 用 明 顯。與 此 同 時，居 民 消 費 需 求 分 化 加     attaches great importance to expanding domestic demand and
劇，商 品 零 售 尤 其 是 高 端 消 費 承 壓 放 緩，服 務     enhancing market confidence. The effective promotion of
消 費 增 勢 穩 健，消 費 者 日 趨 重 視 消 費 價 值。首     consumption policies and the improvement of long-term
批消費基礎設施公募房地產投資信託基金 (REITs)              mechanisms simultaneously have expanded the scale of
成 功 上 市，存 量 資 產 加 速 盤 活，購 物 中 心 運 營     consumption while updating the consumption scenarios, with
迎 來 精 細 化 及 專 業 化 競 爭 紅 利。物 管 行 業 繼 續   consumption playing a vital role. Meanwhile, the differentiation
高 質 量 發 展 主 基 調，政 策 強 化 行 業 規 範 性 並 長   of consumer demand has intensified, while product
期 利 好，頭 部 物 企 持 續 構 築 規 模、效 率 與 服 務     consumption, especially high-end consumption, has slowed
品 質 的 正 向 循 環，並 不 斷 探 索 產 業 鏈 延 伸 專 業   down under pressures. The growth trend of service
細分領域的戰略佈局，謀求更大的成長空間。                    consumption has been steady, and consumers are paying
                                        more attention to the value of consumption. In light of the
                                        successful listing of the first batch of consumer infrastructure
                                        public Real Estate Investment Trusts (REITs), the revitalisation
                                        of existing assets was accelerated, and thus shopping malls
                                        ushered in the competitive advantages of refined and
                                        professional operation. The property management industry
                                        continues to develop with high quality, and the policy
                                        strengthens the standardisation of industry and brings long-
                                        term benefits. Besides, the leading property management
                                        enterprises continue to build a positive cycle of scale,
                                        efficiency and service quality and constantly explore the
                                        strategic layout of the industrial chain to extend professional
                                        subdivisions, thereby seeking greater growth space.

上 市 以 來，外 部 經 營 環 境 不 確 定 性 明 顯 上 升，    Since the listing of the Company, notwithstanding the significant
本 集 團 始 終 堅 持 戰 略 引 領，保 持 戰 略 定 力，以     increase in the uncertainty of the external operating
自身工作的確定性應對形勢變化的不確定性。                    environment, the Group has always adhered to strategic
上半年，本集團堅決貫徹落實「十四五」中期檢                   guidance, maintained strategic focus, and adapted to the
討 成 果，紥 實 推 進 各 項 戰 略 舉 措 走 深 走 實，賽     uncertainty arising from changes in the situation with the
道 細 分 和 精 益 化 的 業 務 策 略 落 地 顯 效，不 斷 增   certainty of its own works. In the first half of the year, the Group
強發展韌性、提升經營質效、鍛造核心能力。                    resolutely implemented the results of the interim review of the
                                        “14th Five-Year Plan”. By solidly and deeply promoting various
                                        strategic initiatives, the Group successfully implemented the
                                        business strategy of refined business segmentation and lean
                                        operation, continuously enhanced its development resilience,
                                        and improved the quality and efficiency of its operations,
                                        thereby forging its core capabilities.

      來自針對開發商提供的非業主增值服務收入為                                    revenue generated from value-added services to non-property

      期降低 16.2%，主要受開發商增量項目獲取及存                                accounting for 4.2% of our total revenue, which decreased by

      營銷服務、前期顧問諮詢等業務收入有所下降。                                   mainly affected by the slowed acquisition of new projects and

      道毛利率為 60.9%，較去年同期增長 2.4 個百分             for the commercial management business was 60.9%,
      點，主 要 得 益 於 商 業 運 營 服 務 規 模 擴 大。經 營     representing an increase of 2.4 percentage points as
      槓 桿 效 應 增 強，收 入 增 長 和 成 本 優 化 共 同 帶 動   compared with the Prior Period. The increase was mainly due
      毛利率水平進一步提升。                             to the expansion of the scale of commercial operation services.
                                              Enhanced operational leverage, revenue growth and cost
                                              optimisation, collectively contributed to the further improvement
                                              of the gross profit margin.

      以 提升 項 目 現場 品 質；(ii) 受 開發 商行 業影 響，      property management services segment. The reasons for such
      非業主增值服務收入（於本集團各分部中毛利率                   reduction were mainly due to: (i) a commitment to a long-term

      下降。                                     site project quality; and (ii) the impact of the developer industry,

      外匯風險                                     FOREIGN CURRENCY RISK
      由 於 本 集 團 業 務 主 要 於 中 國 進 行，本 集 團 主 要    As the Group’s business is mainly conducted in the PRC, we
      採 用 人 民 幣 作 為 結 算 貨 幣。截 至 二 零 二 四 年 六    mainly adopt RMB as the settlement currency. As of 30 June
      月 三 十 日 止，非 人 民 幣 資 產 及 負 債 主 要 為 現 金    2024, non-RMB assets and liabilities mainly included cash of
      港 幣 285.7 百 萬 元、19,410.9 美 元。管 理 層 認 為   HKD285.7 million and United States Dollar (“US$”)19,410.9.
      本 集 團 在 經 營 方 面 並 無 重 大 外 匯 風 險，人 民 幣    The management believes that the operation of the Group was
      匯率波動不會對本集團財務狀況帶來顯著影響。                    not exposed to material foreign currency risk. No significant
      本 集 團 目 前 並 無 外 幣 風 險 對 沖 政 策，然 而，管      impact was caused by the fluctuation of RMB exchange rate on
      理層將對外匯風險敞口實施動態監控並將根據                     the Group’s financial position. Currently, the Group does not
      市場環境的變化進行必要調整。                           have any hedging policies against its foreign exchange risk,
                                               but the management will actively monitor the foreign exchange
                                               exposure and make necessary adjustments in accordance with
                                               the changes in market environment.

      截 至 二 零 二 四 年 六 月 三 十 日 止，本 集 團 在 中 國    As of 30 June 2024, the Group had 40,244 full time employees

      年十二月三十一日：40,977 名）。本集團根據員                and Hong Kong. The Group remunerates its employees based
      工 的 業 績 表 現、工 作 經 驗 和 市 場 工 資 水 平 來 決    on their performance, working experience and market salary
      定員工的薪酬。此外，酌情給予績效獎金，其他                    levels. In addition, performance bonus is granted on a
      員工福利包括公積金、保險與醫療計劃。                       discretionary basis. Other employee benefits include housing

有效的問責性。                                  structures and internal control procedures of the Group so as
                                         to achieve effective accountability.


 本公司已應用上市規則附錄 C1《企業管治守則》                 The Company has applied the principles and adopted the code
（「企業管治守則」）所載的原則並採納當中所述                   provisions stated in the Corporate Governance Code contained
 的 守 則 條 文。本 公 司 堅 信，董 事 會 中 執 行 董 事     in Appendix C1 to the Listing Rules (the “CG Code”). The
 與 獨 立 非 執 行 董 事 的 組 合 應 保 持 平 衡，以 使 董   Company is committed to the view that the Board should
 事會有強大的獨立性，能夠有效作出獨立判斷。                   include a balanced composition of executive Directors and
                                         independent non-executive Directors so that there is a strong

                                         exercise independent judgment.

      報告包括主要向負責財務和會計事務的人員作                    the entity , issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public

      的範圍遠較根據香港審計準則進行審核的範圍                    making enquiries, primarily of persons responsible for financial
      為 小，故 不 能 令 吾 等 保 證 將 知 悉 在 審 核 中 可 能   and accounting matters, and applying analytical and other
      被 發 現 的 所 有 重 大 事 項。因 此，吾 等 不 會 發 表     review procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than
      審核意見。                                   an audit conducted in accordance with Hong Kong Standards

            (a)   本集團的估值程序                                         (a)    Valuation processes of the Group

                  本集團投資物業位於中國內地。本集團按                                      The Group’s investment properties are situated in Chinese
                  公允價值計量其投資物業。本集團投資物                                      Mainland. The Group measures its investment properties
                  業於二零二四年六月三十日的公允價值                                       at fair value. The fair value of the Group’s investment
                  乃基於獨立專業合資格估值師仲量聯行                                       properties as at 30 June 2024 have been determined on
                  企業評估及諮詢有限公司進行的估值釐定。                                     the basis of valuation carried out by Jones Lang LaSalle
                                                                          Corporate Appraisal and Advisory Limited, an independent
                                                                          and professionally qualified valuer.

            (b)   估值技術                                             (b)    Valuation techniques

                  估值中採用的估值方法為收入法及剩餘法。                                     The valuation methodology adopted in valuation are the
                                                                          income approach and residual method.

                  收入法 — 收入法的運作考慮來自現有租                                     Income approach — the income approach operates by
                  賃的物業租賃收入（就潛在的租賃復歸收                                      considering the rental income of the property derived from
                  入作出適當撥備），然後以適當的資本化                                      the existing tenancy with due allowance for the
                  率資本化為價值。                                                reversionary income potential of the tenancy, which is then
                                                                          capitalised into the value at an appropriate capitalisation
                                                                          rate.

          剩餘法 — 剩餘法考慮總發展價值（「總發                              Residual method — the residual approach takes into
          展 價 值」）並 扣 除 尚 未 發 生 的 建 築 成 本、                   account the residual value of the gross development value
          開支以及發展利潤後的剩餘價值。剩餘法                                (“GDV”) after deduction of the outstanding construction
          首先計算假設建議發展項目於估值日已                                 costs and expenses as well as profit element. It involves
          落成的合計總發展價值。建議發展項目的                                firstly the assessment of the aggregate GDV of the
          估計成本包括建築成本、市場推廣支出、                                proposed development, as if completed, as at the
          專業費用、財務費用以及相關成本，另加                                valuation date. Estimated costs of the proposed
          發展商的風險及利潤撥備。尚未發生的開                                development include construction costs, marketing
          發成本將由總發展價值中扣除，並得出標                                expense, professional fee, finance charges and
          的物業的剩餘價值。                                         associated costs, plus an allowance for the developer’s

       10 貿易應收款項及應收票據（續）                                     10 TRADE AND BILL RECEIVABLES
                                                                (continued)
            貿易應收款項及應收票據主要因按包乾制                                     Trade and bill receivables mainly arise from property
            管理的物業管理服務及增值服務而產生。                                     management services managed on a lump sum basis
                                                                   and value-added services.


            包乾制物業管理服務收入按照相關物業服                                     Property management services income on a lump sum
            務協議的條款收取。物業管理服務的服務                                     basis are received in accordance with the term of the
            收入須由業主在提供服務時支付。本集團                                     relevant property service agreements. Service income
            力求嚴格控制其未收回的應收款項。逾期                                     from property management services is due for payment
            結餘由管理層定期審閱。鑒於上文所述，                                     by the property owners upon rendering of services. The
